## Account

### createAccountImage

`account.createAccountImage`

Create an Account Image

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.shipengine.api.account.createAccountImage({
	body: { name: 'example', image_content_type: 'image/png', image_data: 'iVBORw0KGgo=' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

The provider expects image_data as a Base64-encoded string in a JSON body (PNG or JPEG); this is not a multipart upload.

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  name: string,
  is_default?: boolean,
  image_content_type: image/png | image/jpeg,
  image_data: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `label_image_id` | `string` | No | — |
| `name` | `string` | No | — |
| `is_default` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `image_content_type` | `image/png \| image/jpeg` | No | — |
| `image_data` | `string` | No | — |
| `created_at` | `string` | No | — |
| `modified_at` | `string` | No | — |



---

### deleteAccountImageById

`account.deleteAccountImageById`

Delete Account Image By Id

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.shipengine.api.account.deleteAccountImageById({
	path: { label_image_id: 'img_DtBXupDBxREpHnwEXhTfgK' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |

Delete Account Image By Id deletes, disconnects, cancels, or otherwise changes an account resource in a way that may be difficult to reverse. Verify the target before calling.

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
  label_image_id: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `string \| undefined`


---

### listAccountImages

`account.listAccountImages`

List Account Images

**Risk:** `read`

**Output**

| `total` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `page` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `pages` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `links` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `images` | `object[]` | Yes | — |

```ts
    href?: string,
    type?: string
```

## Addresses

### parseAddress

`addresses.parseAddress`

Parse an address

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.shipengine.api.addresses.parseAddress({
	body: { text: 'Example shipment from 123 Example St, Example City, CA 94000' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  text: string,
  address?: {
    name?: string,
    phone?: string,
    email?: string | null,
    company_name?: string | null,
    address_line1?: string,
    address_line2?: string | null,
    address_line3?: string | null,
    city_locality?: string,
    state_province?: string,
    postal_code?: string,
    country_code?: string,
    address_residential_indicator?: unknown | yes | no
  }
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `score` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `address` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `entities` | `object[]` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="address full type">

```ts
{
  name?: string,
  phone?: string,
  email?: string | null,
  company_name?: string | null,
  address_line1?: string,
  address_line2?: string | null,
  address_line3?: string | null,
  city_locality?: string,
  state_province?: string,
  postal_code?: string,
  country_code?: string,
  address_residential_indicator?: unknown | yes | no
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="entities full type">

```ts
{
  type: string,
  score: number,
  text: string,
  start_index: number,
  end_index: number,
  result?: Record<string, JsonValue>
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### validateAddress

`addresses.validateAddress`

Validate An Address

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.shipengine.api.addresses.validateAddress({
	body: [{
		address_line1: '123 Example St',
		city_locality: 'Example City',
		state_province: 'CA',
		country_code: 'US',
	}],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `body` | `object[]` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  name?: string,
  phone?: string,
  email?: string | null,
  company_name?: string | null,
  address_line1: string,
  address_line2?: string | null,
  address_line3?: string | null,
  city_locality: string,
  state_province: string,
  postal_code?: string,
  country_code: string,
  address_residential_indicator?: unknown | yes | no
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  status: unverified | verified | warning | error,
  original_address: {
    name: string,
    phone?: string,
    email?: string | null,
    company_name?: string | null,
    address_line1: string,
    address_line2?: string | null,
    address_line3?: string | null,
    city_locality: string,
    state_province: string,
    postal_code: string,
    country_code?: string,
    address_residential_indicator?: unknown | yes | no
  },
  matched_address: {
    name: string,
    phone?: string,
    email?: string | null,
    company_name?: string | null,
    address_line1: string,
    address_line2?: string | null,
    address_line3?: string | null,
    city_locality: string,
    state_province: string,
    postal_code: string,
    country_code?: string,
    address_residential_indicator?: unknown | yes | no
  } | null,
  messages: {
    code: a1000 | a1001 | a1002 | a1003 | a1004 | a1005 | a1006 | a1007 | a1008 | r1000 | r1001 | r1002 | r1003,
    message: string,
    type: error | warning | info,
    detail_code: unsupported_country | non_supported_country | minimum_postal_code_verification_failed | street_does_not_match_unique_street_name | multiple_directionals | multiple_matches | suite_not_valid | suite_missing | incompatible_paired_labels | invalid_house_number | missing_house_number | invalid_box_number | invalid_charge_event | missing_box_number | missing_cmra_or_private_mail_box_number | suite_has_no_secondaries | postal_code_changed_or_added | state_province_changed_or_added | city_locality_changed_or_added | urbanization_changed | street_name_spelling_changed_or_added | street_name_type_changed_or_added | street_direction_changed_or_added | suite_type_changed_or_added | suite_unit_number_changed_or_added | double_dependent_locality_changed_or_added | subadministrative_area_changed_or_added | subnational_area_changed_or_added | po_box_changed_or_added | premise_type_changed_or_added | house_number_changed | organization_changed_or_added | partially_verified_to_state_level | partially_verified_to_city_level | partially_verified_to_street_level | partially_verified_to_premise_level | verified_to_state_level | verified_to_city_level | verified_to_street_level | verified_to_premise_level | verified_to_suite_level | coded_to_street_lavel | coded_to_neighborhood_level | coded_to_community_level | coded_to_state_level | coded_to_rooftop_level | coded_to_rooftop_interpolation_level | name_max_length_exceeded | phone_max_length_exceeded | company_name_max_length_exceeded | line1_min_max_length | line2_max_length_exceeded | line3_max_length_exceeded | city_locality_max_length_exceeded | state_province_max_length_exceeded | invalid_postal_code | country_invalid_length | address_not_found | null
  }[]
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### createBatch

`batches.createBatch`

Create a batch. Setting process_labels.create_batch_and_process_labels purchases labels and may charge the account.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.shipengine.api.batches.createBatch({
	body: { external_batch_id: 'se-28529731' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

The optional process_labels.create_batch_and_process_labels flag processes label purchases as part of batch creation and can incur carrier charges. This endpoint is marked destructive.

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  external_batch_id?: string,
  batch_notes?: string,
  shipment_ids?: string[],
  rate_ids?: string[]
} | {
  external_batch_id?: string,
  batch_notes?: string,
  shipment_ids?: string[],
  rate_ids?: string[],
  process_labels?: {
    create_batch_and_process_labels?: boolean,
    ship_date?: string,
    label_layout?: 4x6 | letter | A4 | A6,
    label_format?: pdf | png | zpl,
    display_scheme?: label | paperless | label_and_paperless
  }
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `label_layout` | `4x6 \| letter \| A4 \| A6` | Yes | — |
| `label_format` | `pdf \| png \| zpl` | Yes | — |
| `batch_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `batch_number` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `external_batch_id` | `string \| null` | Yes | — |
| `batch_notes` | `string \| null` | Yes | — |
| `created_at` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `processed_at` | `string \| null` | Yes | — |
| `errors` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `process_errors` | `object[]` | Yes | — |
| `warnings` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `completed` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `forms` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `count` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `batch_shipments_url` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `batch_labels_url` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `batch_errors_url` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `label_download` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `form_download` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `paperless_download` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `status` | `open \| queued \| processing \| completed \| completed_with_errors \| archived \| notifying \| invalid` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="process_errors full type">

```ts
{
  error_source: carrier | order_source | shipengine,
  error_type: account_status | business_rules | validation | security | system | integrations,
  error_code: auto_fund_not_supported | batch_cannot_be_modified | carrier_conflict | carrier_disconnected | carrier_not_connected | carrier_not_supported | confirmation_not_supported | default_warehouse_cannot_be_deleted | field_conflict | field_value_required | forbidden | identifier_conflict | identifiers_must_match | insufficient_funds | invalid_address | invalid_billing_plan | invalid_field_value | invalid_identifier | invalid_status | invalid_string_length | label_images_not_supported | meter_failure | order_source_not_active | rate_limit_exceeded | refresh_not_supported | request_body_required | return_label_not_supported | settings_not_supported | subscription_inactive | terms_not_accepted | tracking_not_supported | trial_expired | unauthorized | unknown | unspecified | verification_failure | warehouse_conflict | webhook_event_type_conflict | customs_items_required | incompatible_paired_labels | invalid_charge_event | invalid_object | no_rates_returned | file_not_found | shipping_rule_not_found | service_not_determined | funding_source_registration_in_progress | insurance_failure | funding_source_missing_configuration | funding_source_error,
  message: string,
  carrier_id?: string,
  carrier_code?: string,
  field_name?: string
}[]
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="batch_shipments_url full type">

```ts
{
  href?: string,
  type?: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="batch_labels_url full type">

```ts
{
  href?: string,
  type?: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="batch_errors_url full type">

```ts
{
  href?: string,
  type?: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="label_download full type">

```ts
{
  href?: string,
  pdf?: string,
  png?: string,
  zpl?: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="form_download full type">

```ts
{
  href?: string,
  type?: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="paperless_download full type">

```ts
{
  href?: string,
  instructions?: string | null,
  handoff_code?: string | null
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### processBatch

`batches.processBatch`

Process Batch ID Labels

```ts
	path: { batch_id: 'se-28529731' },
	body: {},
});
```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |

## Downloads

### downloadFile

`downloads.downloadFile`

Download File

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.shipengine.api.downloads.downloadFile({
	path: { subdir: 'example', filename: 'example', dir: 'example' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `query` | `object` | No | — |

Binary download uses a custom byte-preserving transport because the shared Corsair JSON transport decodes binary bodies as text. The validated return value is Base64 with a 50 MiB safety limit.

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
{
  subdir: string,
  filename: string,
  dir: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="query full type">

```ts
{
  download?: string,
  rotation?: number
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `kind` | `binary` | Yes | — |
| `contentType` | `application/pdf \| image/png \| application/zpl` | Yes | — |
| `base64` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `byteLength` | `number` | Yes | — |

### createLabel

`labels.createLabel`

Purchase Label

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.shipengine.api.labels.createLabel({
	body: {
		shipment: {
			carrier_id: 'se-28529731',
			service_code: 'usps_first_class_mail',
			ship_to: {
				name: 'example',
				address_line1: '123 Example St',
				city_locality: 'Example City',
				state_province: 'CA',
				postal_code: '94000',
			},
			packages: [{
				weight: { value: 0, unit: 'pound' },
			}],
		},
	},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

Purchase Label purchases one or more carrier labels and can incur shipping charges. ShipStation wallet-carrier costs are deducted from the account balance; merchant carrier accounts follow carrier billing terms. Verify the rate before using a production key.

### createLabelFromRate

`labels.createLabelFromRate`

Purchase Label with Rate ID

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.shipengine.api.labels.createLabelFromRate({
	path: { rate_id: 'se-28529731' },
	body: {},
});
```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |

### compareBulkRates

`rates.compareBulkRates`

Get Bulk Rates

**Risk:** `read`

```ts

	body: {
		rate_options: {
			carrier_ids: ['se-28529731'],
		},
		shipment_ids: ['se-28529731'],
	},
});
```

### parseShipment

`shipments.parseShipment`

Parse shipping info

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.shipengine.api.shipments.parseShipment({
	body: { text: 'Example shipment from 123 Example St, Example City, CA 94000' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

### untagShipment

`shipments.untagShipment`

Remove Tag from Shipment

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.shipengine.api.shipments.untagShipment({
	path: { shipment_id: 'se-28529731', tag_name: 'example' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `path` | `object` | Yes | — |

Remove Tag from Shipment deletes, disconnects, cancels, or otherwise changes an account resource in a way that may be difficult to reverse. Verify the target before calling.

<Accordion title="path full type">

```ts
}
```

### updateShipment

`shipments.updateShipment`

Update Shipment By ID

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.shipengine.api.shipments.updateShipment({
	path: { shipment_id: 'se-28529731' },
	body: {
		ship_to: {
			name: 'example',
			address_line1: '123 Example St',
			city_locality: 'Example City',
			state_province: 'CA',
			postal_code: '94000',

		ship_from: {
			name: 'example',
			address_line1: '123 Example St',

});
```
